38 //======================================================================================================================
39 //! @defgroup reverse_horner kyosu::reverse_horner
40 //! @ingroup agnostic
41 //! @brief Implement the reverse_horner scheme to evaluate polynomials
42 //! @{
43 //! @var reverse_horner
44 //!
45 //! If \f$(a_i)_{0\le i\le n-1}\f$ denotes the coefficients of the polynomial by increasing
46 //! power order, the reverse Horner scheme evaluates the polynom \f$p\f$ at \f$x\f$ by :
47 //! \f$\displaystyle p(x) = (((a_{n-1}x+a_{n-2})x+ ... )x + a_0)\f$.\n
48 //! For non commutative cases it is a left-reverse_horner scheme: coefficients are
49 //! at the left of the x powers.
50 //!
51 //! using the `right` semantic modifier allows to use a right-reverse Horner scheme:
52 //! coefficients are at the right of the x powers).
53 //!
54 //! @groupheader{Header file}
55 //!
56 //! @code
57 //! #include <kyosu/functions.hpp>
58 //! @endcode
59 //!
60 //! @groupheader{Callable Signatures}
61 //!
62 //! @code
63 //! namespace kyosu
64 //! {
65 //! template< auto T, auto C ...> auto reverse_horner(T x, C ... coefs) noexcept; //1
66 //! template< auto C, auto K> auto reverse_horner(T x, K tup) noexcept; //2
67 //!
68 //! // Semantic Modifiers
69 //! template<auto T, auto C ...> auto reverse_horner[left](T x, C ... coefs) noexcept; //1
70 //! template<auto C, auto K> auto reverse_horner[left]r(T x, K tup) noexcept; //2
71 //! template<auto T, auto C ...> auto reverse_horner[right](T x, C ... coefs) noexcept; //3
72 //! template<auto C, auto K> auto reverse_horner[right]r(T x, K tup) noexcept; //3
73 //!
74 //! }
75 //! @endcode
76 //!
77 //!! **Parameters**
78 //!
79 //! * `x` : real or cayley-dickson argument.
80 //!
81 //! * `coefs...` : real or cayley-dickson arguments. The coefficients by increasing power order
82 //!
83 //! * `tup` : kumi tuple containing The coefficients by increasing power order.
84 //!
85 //! **Return value**
86 //!
87 //! 1. Polynom is evaluated at x the other inputs are the polynomial coefficients.
88 //! The value of the polynom at `x` is returned, according to the formula:
89 //! \f$\displaystyle p(x) = (((a_{n-1}x+a_{n-2})x+ ... )x + a_0)\f$.\n
90 //! For non commutative cases it is a left-reverse_horner scheme.
91 //! 2. Polynom is evaluated at x the other input is a kumi tuple containing the coefficients
92 //! 3. the right modifyier is useful only when dealing wirh cayley-dickson non commutative algebras.\n
93 //! The value of the polynom at `x` is returned, according to the formula:
94 //! \f$\displaystyle p(x) = (x (x (x a_{n-1}+a_{n-2})+ ... )+a_0)\f$.\n
95 //! Of course for real or complex entries left and right have no specific actions
96 //!
97 //! **Notes**
98 //!
99 //! If the coefficients are simd values of cardinal N, this means you simultaneously
100 //! compute the values of N polynomials.
101 //! * If x is scalar, the polynomials are all computed at the same point
102 //! * If x is simd, the nth polynomial is computed on the nth value of x
103 //!
104 //! @groupheader{External references}
105 //! * [Wikipedia: Horner's method](https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Horner%27s_method)
106 //!
107 //! @groupheader{Example}
108 //! @godbolt{doc/reverse_horner.cpp}
109 //======================================================================================================================
110 inline constexpr auto reverse_horner = eve::functor<reverse_horner_t>;
111 //======================================================================================================================
112 //! @}
113 //======================================================================================================================
114 }
115
116 namespace kyosu::_
117 {
118 template<typename X, eve::non_empty_product_type Data, eve::callable_options O>
119 63107 KYOSU_FORCEINLINE constexpr auto reverse_horner_(KYOSU_DELAY(), O const& o, X x, coefficients<Data> const& t) noexcept
120 {
121 126214 return horner[o](x, coefficients(kumi::reverse(t)));
122 }
123
124 template<typename X, typename... Zs, eve::callable_options O>
125 20234 KYOSU_FORCEINLINE constexpr auto reverse_horner_(KYOSU_DELAY(), O const& o, X xx, Zs... zs) noexcept
126 {
127 using r_t = kyosu::as_cayley_dickson_like_t<X, Zs...>;
128 20234 auto x = convert(xx, eve::as_element<r_t>());
129 20234 return reverse_horner[o](x, coefficients{convert(zs, eve::as_element<r_t>())...});
130 }
